still knew they were doing it.\n- key: S020\n  name: Hallucinations\n  description: Perception-like experiences that occur without an external stimulus and are as vivid and clear as normal perception.\n  manifestation: I came in because I keep hearing voices clear as somebody standing in my little studio, saying my name and telling me my work is being judged, even when the house is quiet and nobody is there.\n- key: S022\n  name: Disorganized_Abnormal_Behavior\n  description: A state where behavior and motor control are severely disturbed, appearing as movements or reactions inappropriate to the situation.\n  manifestation: There have been times on client visits where I’d stop what I was supposed to be doing and start lining up gloves, pill cups, and crochet scraps in patterns, or stand in the hallway staring like I was waiting for some signal.\n- key: S023\n  name: Negative_Symptoms\n  description: A state where emotions, motivation, speech, or social interest that should normally be present are diminished or lost.\n  manifestation: When I’m not all wound up, I can sit for hours with my coffee getting cold, not answering calls, not going to the walking club, and not even caring to pick up my paintbrush.\n- key: S033\n  name: Elevated_Irritable_Mood\n  description: A distinct period of abnormally and persistently elevated, expansive, or irritable mood.\n  manifestation: I go from feeling so bright and chosen that Greenville can’t hold all my plans to snapping hard at someone over a small thing, like a client asking me to repeat myself.\n- key: S034\n  name: Increased_Activity\n  description: Abnormally and persistently increased goal-directed activity or energy.\n  manifestation: I get so much energy that I’ll clean my whole kitchen, start three paintings, crochet half a blanket, and rewrite care schedules all in the same night without being able to sit down.\n- key: S035\n  name: Inflated_Self_Esteem\n  description: Inflated self-esteem or grandiosity (may range from uncritical self-confidence to delusional proportions).\n  manifestation: I truly believed my artwork was going to get discovered any day and that I was supposed to build a whole new care-and-art program that doctors and agencies would come learn from me.\n- key: S036\n  name: Decreased_Sleep\n  description: Decreased need for sleep (e.g., feels rested after only 3 hours of sleep).\n  manifestation: I’ve gone many nights sleeping maybe two or three hours, then getting up feeling charged like I had a full rest and going straight back to painting or making calls.\n- key: S038\n  name: Racing_Thoughts\n  description: Flight of ideas or subjective experience that thoughts are racing.\n  manifestation: My thoughts run so fast that one minute I’m thinking about a client’s medicine, then rent, then a painting title, then some message in a song, and I can’t slow it down enough to make sense.\n- key: S039\n  name: outside, a bird at the window, or a color on the table pulls me away and I’m off doing something else before I know it.\n- key: additional_requirement_1\n  name: Additional Requirement 1\n duration of the active and residual portions of the illness\n  manifestation: For most of this stretch, I was either wound up with big plans, little sleep, and nonstop activity, or coming down from that but still not back to myself.\n- key: additional_requirement_3\n  name: Additional Requirement 3\n  description: delusions_and_hallucinations occur for 2 or more weeks in the absence of a major mood episode\n  manifestation: There was a time after my energy dropped and I was sleeping more normally when the voices and the belief that people were judging or tracking my work kept going for That's just... what happened.\n\nDoctor: Can you tell me more about times when you felt like others were watching you or trying to interfere with your life? How often does this happen, and does it seem to be related to how you're feeling emotionally at the time?\n\nPatient: I don't know... it's hard to say how often. \n\nI guess... well, there was a time when I finally stopped feeling so wound up. My energy dropped back down, and I was actually sleeping like a normal person again. But... it didn't stop. The voices, I mean. And that feeling that they were tracking my work and judging me. \n\nEven when I wasn't feeling all over the place emotionally... it just kept going. For a few weeks, at least. That's... all I really remember about it.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any periods where you felt down, depressed, or hopeless for most of the day, nearly every day, for at least two weeks in a row?\n\nPatient: No... I don't really get like that. I mean... maybe just tired from work sometimes, but... no. Not depressed like you're saying. I don't think so.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any difficulties taking care of yourself, keeping up with your work, or getting along with others because of these experiences?\n\nPatient: I guess... things got a bit hard to manage. \n\nI was missing shifts at my aide job... getting the care notes all mixed up. I just couldn't keep my visits straight anymore. They said my job was in danger. \n\nAnd... I just stayed home mostly. I stopped going to my walking club, and... I didn't go to the library art talks anymore either. Just... stayed by myself.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a period where you felt so down, empty, or hopeless that it was hard to function for most of the day, nearly every day, for at least two weeks?\n\nPatient: I thought... didn't you just ask me that? Like I said before... no. I don't get down or empty like that. Just... tired from work sometimes. That's it.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever experienced a time when you felt a loss of pleasure in activities you usually enjoy, or found it hard to feel anything positive, even when good things happened?\n\nPatient: No... I don't think so. I still like things fine. \n\nI know I said I stopped going to my club and the library, but... it wasn't because I couldn't enjoy them. I just... didn't want to go out. \n\nSo... no. I don't have that problem.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a period of time when you felt unusually energetic, needed less sleep, or felt like your thoughts were racing, where others might have noticed a change in your behavior?\n\nPatient: Well... sometimes, I guess. \n\nThere are nights where I just... I don't know, I get a lot done. I'll clean the whole kitchen, start working on three different paintings, crochet half a blanket... I even tried to rewrite the care schedules for work. All in one night. \n\nI just... couldn't sit down. Had too much energy, I suppose.\n\nDoctor: Can you tell me if there were times when your thoughts were going so fast you couldn’t keep up, or when you were so energetic that you felt like you didn’t need to sleep?\n\nPatient: Well... I guess so. Sometimes. \n\nLike... my mind just won't stop. I'll be thinking about a client's medicine, and then suddenly I'm worrying about rent, and then I'm trying to come up with a title for a painting, and then... I don't know, trying to figure out some message in a song on the radio. It all just goes so fast. I can't really slow it down to make sense of any of it. \n\nSo... yes, I suppose.\n\nDoctor: Were there times when your thoughts were so fast that other people noticed you were talking more quickly or jumping from topic to topic, or when you felt so full of energy that others thought you were acting unusually wired or out of control?\n\nPatient: I... suppose people might have noticed. \n\nSometimes I just... I get this feeling. Like everything is just so bright, and I'm... chosen for something. Like all my plans are just too big for Greenville, you know? \n\nBut then... I don't know, it shifts. I'll get so short with people. Like if a client asks me to repeat myself, I just... snap at them. Hard. It's just... a lot of back and forth, I guess.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a period lasting several days or more where you felt extremely energetic, talkative, or needed very little sleep, and others might have said you were acting out of control or not yourself?\n\nPatient: I suppose... yes. There are times like that. \n\nI'll go... I don't know, maybe a few days, a week... sleeping just two or three hours a night. But I don't feel tired. I'll wake up feeling... charged, I guess. Like I had a full night's rest. \n\nThen I just... get up and go right back to my paintings, or start making phone calls. It just... happens sometimes.\n\nDoctor: When you were not feeling unusually energetic or wired, did you still have the experiences of hearing voices or believing that others were watching or trying to interfere with you?\n\nPatient: Yes... I think so. \n\nEven when the energy kind of... faded away, and I started sleeping normally again... those other things didn't stop. \n\nThe voices kept going. And that feeling... that people were watching me, tracking what I was doing with my work, judging it... that lasted for a few weeks after I calmed down. It just... stayed with me.\n\nDoctor: During the times when you weren't feeling unusually energetic or down, did the voices or the belief that others were watching you continue without any connection to how you were feeling emotionally?\n\nPatient: I... yes. Like I said before. \n\nWhen the energy dropped... and I was sleeping more normally again... those things just kept going. For several weeks, I guess. The voices, and... the feeling that people were tracking my work, judging it. \n\nIt didn't seem to matter that I wasn't... wired anymore. It just kept happening.\n\nDoctor: Have you had times when your ability to function at work, take care of yourself, or interact with others was clearly worse, even when you weren't feeling unusually energetic or down emotionally?\n\nPatient: I guess... yes, things got harder. \n\nI missed some shifts at my job as an aide. I... couldn't keep my care notes straight, or organize my visits like I normally do. They said my job was in danger. \n\nAnd... I just didn't go out much. I stopped going to my walking club. Didn't go to the art talks at the library anymore. I just... stayed home by myself.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had experiences where you heard voices or believed that others were watching or interfering with you, even when you were not feeling unusually energetic, depressed, or emotionally high or low?\n\nPatient: I... yes. I think we just went over this. \n\nBut... yes. Like I said, after my energy dropped... and I was sleeping normally again... it didn't stop. The voices... and thinking people were tracking my work, judging me... that kept going for several weeks. \n\nI don't really know what else to say about it. It just kept happening.\n\nDoctor: Have the voices or the feeling that others were watching or judging you lasted for more than a month without stopping?\n\nPatient: Yes... a lot longer than a month. \n\nI guess... it's been about nine months now. That they've been going on. The voices, and... worrying about my work. It just hasn't stopped."
